Citation

Registration (Details of Registered Contractors) Notice, 2024
and shall be deemed to have come into operation on the 1st of
January, 2024.
Registered

contractors
set out in the Schedules to this Notice.
(2) The public is hereby notified that the companies
whose names and details appear in the Schedule to this
Notice are registered by Contractors Registration Board.
